These behaviours are undefined in this GDD or in a neighbour, so a criterion cannot be written until the owner answers; each is also an Open Question.

- ~~**PATCH on a subject whose `hp` already exceeds `PATCH_CAP_BP`**~~ — closed: refused `ABOVE_PATCH_CAP`, `hp` never clamped down (LS-18 M7; OQ-9 closed) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
- ~~**`tool_suppressant` at 0 charges**~~ — closed *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*: the tool is dormant and outside the MVP roster (X6, OQ-29 resolved); AC-109 is the lint.
- ~~**The loose-world holder and the instance-id allocator**~~ — closed for ownership: Interaction owns `loose[]` and the counter (IA-28); Suit mints no ids (OQ-20 answered) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. Closed *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*: IA-28 `loose[]` carries the six-field instance, so AC-99 and AC-117 run against the real holder (OQ-47 resolved).
- **The hull-plate replacement path** for a DESTROYED HULL_SEGMENT (M5 accepts only ABSENT or SALVAGED slots) — bounced back by the Graph (its Open Question 27: "the Graph consumes no item at M5"): the Item DB's answer is `repair_part_for(NODE 50)` = `part_hull_plate` x 1; whether the install consumes it is Assembly's `install_module` (OQ-1, OQ-11).
- ~~**FULL repair on a SEAM** (`subject_kind` 4)~~ — closed: held at `NONE` and the Graph confirmed a FULL on a SEAM is not offered at MVP (LS-18 M7; OQ-12 closed) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
- ~~**The reinstall mechanism for cap and clock**~~ — closed: M6 returns `hp_cap` and `patched_at_step` and M5 takes them (LS-18), so AC-32a to AC-32f are no longer PROVISIONAL (OQ-10 closed) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
- ~~**`T_CYCLE_MIN` 100 s and the salvage-reinstall break-even**~~ — withdrawn with F-15; AC-62 and AC-94 are withdrawn *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
- **Stock above a live `stock_cap` on restore** — the Graph's restore behaviour (OQ-31); AC-105 is BLOCKED-ON it.
- **The shed verb and Suit's holder mutator** — Suit and Interaction (OQ-34); AC-107 and AC-108 are BLOCKED-ON them.
- ~~**No minimum-spec hardware in `project.yaml`**~~ — closed: RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK are named and recorded as `qa.min_spec` (delegated ruling R12); the wall-clock budgets of AC-68 and AC-69 are gated on them at the spike, pending spike measurement *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12)*.
- **No `tests/` tree and no gdUnit4 in the repository** although the project's test command points at it; the criteria name files under `tests/unit/item_db/` and `tests/integration/item_db/` that cannot run until the framework is scaffolded (`/test-setup`).
- **Subjective Visual/Feel items** (AC-33, AC-47, AC-53, the Feel Acceptance Criteria) can only be judged by a person with a retained screenshot and a lead; no automation is claimed.

Owner: **user** = a creative or scope decision for the project owner (the autonomous run chose the stated default and logged it; as of 2026-10-28 every such item is decided, by the user's own ruling or by delegated ruling R20/R21, because the user delegated all remaining calls *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R21)*); **agent** = a named sibling GDD or specialist; **GDD** = this document's own pending work. Target: **playtest**, **next GDD** (the named neighbour when it is written), or **ADR**. Items 1 to 6, 29 and 32 were the user-decision items (29 and 32 were marked **USER DECISION** and are **resolved 2026-10-27 by user ruling (design-review decisions)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; items 1 to 5 are resolved 2026-10-28 by delegated rulings R20/R21, so none remains open *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R21)*). Items 8 to 14 were routed to the Graph owner; *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: items 7 to 10, 12, 13 and 22 are closed on the Graph's 2026-10-27 amendment, cited by rule id (LS-18 M5/M6/M7, LS-23, the Item DB row of the Graph's Interactions table); item 11 is bounced back and stays open; item 14 is partly closed)*. New items 31 to 45 come from the design review. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: new items 46 to 55 come from the re-review. No item in this list reverses a user ruling.)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12: X14 is closed; the reference machines RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK are named in `project.yaml` `qa.min_spec`, and R11 is superseded.)*

Binding ask of the Graph owner: on restore, the excess moves to the section's `discarded_*` counter (conserved) and is logged; never a refusal, never silent. Stock is a Graph node field, so the Item DB states the requirement (IDB-6) and AC-105 tests it, BLOCKED-ON the Graph. | agent (Graph owner) | next consistency pass |
